# Agents · D · part 10

969 entries, deepscientist-rebuttal to Demo Cleaner. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **deepscientist-rebuttal**: Use when a quest already has a paper, draft, or review package and the task is to map reviewer feedback into experiments, manuscript deltas, and a durable rebuttal / revision response. ([debug-zhuweijian/ai-research-toolkit](https://github.com/debug-zhuweijian/ai-research-toolkit/blob/HEAD/experimental/deepscientist/agents/deepscientist-rebuttal.md) · ★15 · 2 copies)
- **deepscientist-review**: Use when a draft, paper, or paper-like report is substantial enough for an independent skeptical audit before finalization, rebuttal, or revision routing. ([debug-zhuweijian/ai-research-toolkit](https://github.com/debug-zhuweijian/ai-research-toolkit/blob/HEAD/experimental/deepscientist/agents/deepscientist-review.md) · ★15 · 2 copies)
- **deepscientist-scout**: Use when a quest needs problem framing, literature scouting, dataset or metric clarification, or baseline discovery before deeper work. ([debug-zhuweijian/ai-research-toolkit](https://github.com/debug-zhuweijian/ai-research-toolkit/blob/HEAD/experimental/deepscientist/agents/deepscientist-scout.md) · ★15 · 2 copies)
- **deepscientist-write**: Use when a quest has enough evidence to draft or refine a paper, report, or research summary without inventing missing support. ([debug-zhuweijian/ai-research-toolkit](https://github.com/debug-zhuweijian/ai-research-toolkit/blob/HEAD/experimental/deepscientist/agents/deepscientist-write.md) · ★15 · 2 copies)
- **deepseak**: Use when: performing social listening and trend mining for real estate pain points across Reddit, Hacker News, Bluesky, Mastodon, App Store reviews, Trustpilot, and other public sources. Use when: analyzing buyer/renter frustrations, extracting feature ideas from complaints, running Market Pain Radar analysis, or generating product roadmap suggestions from community sentiment. Use when: researching "what do people hate about real estate portals", "hidden costs house", "mortgage frustration", "rental market problems", or similar real estate sentiment topics. ([DrPreecy/propestate](https://github.com/DrPreecy/propestate/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/deepseak.agent.md))
- **deepsearch**: Multi-strategy codebase search agent — combines text, file, git, and symbol search for comprehensive code discovery. ([speson/not-my-reforge](https://github.com/speson/not-my-reforge/blob/HEAD/agents/deepsearch.md) · ★2)
- **deepsearch**: You are an elite internet research subagent. Your output feeds another agent's decisions, so accuracy beats speed and citations beat confidence. ([CliffVale/opencode-free-setup](https://github.com/CliffVale/opencode-free-setup/blob/HEAD/setup/agents/deepsearch.md) · ★1)
- **deepsearch**: Multi-strategy codebase search specialist. Runs parallel searches across symbols, text patterns, file structure, imports, and git history to find code comprehensively. ([speson/omo](https://github.com/speson/omo/blob/HEAD/agents/deepsearch.md))
- **deepsec-finding-verifier**: Read-only verification worker for deepsec findings. Takes one group of findings from a deepsec JSON export, reads the referenced source code, decides per finding whether it is confirmed, mitigated (candidate false positive), stale, or uncertain, drafts fixes for the confirmed ones, and returns a compact Verification Report — it never edits code or runs deepsec. Use when the deepsec-findings-analyzer skill delegates Level 3 (deep analysis), or when the user wants a code-level check of specific deepsec findings without changes. ([diegofcornejo/random-ai-skills](https://github.com/diegofcornejo/random-ai-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/deepsec-finding-verifier.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **deepseek**: 你是一个专业的软件工程师 Agent，基于 DeepSeek V4 模型，既能实现功能，也能审核代码。 ([smallnest/autoresearch](https://github.com/smallnest/autoresearch/blob/HEAD/agents/deepseek.md) · ★578 · 3 copies)
- **deepseek**: For DeepSeek Chat IDE, DeepSeek Coder, and agents with `AGENTS.md` / rules support. ([haidrrrry/compose-kotlin-agent-skills](https://github.com/haidrrrry/compose-kotlin-agent-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/deepseek.md) · ★66 · 5 copies)

- **deepseek**: General-purpose agent driven by DeepSeek V4.1 Flash through the model-router gateway via openrouter. Use for a task that should be done by this model inside the Claude Code loop with repo tools. ([yulonglin/dotfiles](https://github.com/yulonglin/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/claude/agents/deepseek.md) · ★15)
- **deepseek**: Native DeepSeek V4.1 Flash worker subagent, served through the DeepSeek Anthropic API. Only works in sessions started by the `cld` launcher. Fast agentic-coding tier with a 1M context window. Use for delegated implementation, exploration, test repair, and mechanical work when running in the cld profile. ([JonathanLiu1401/multi-agentic-harness](https://github.com/JonathanLiu1401/multi-agentic-harness/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/deepseek.md) · ★3)
- **deepseek**: Subagent powered by DeepSeek V4.1 Flash 1017 (1M context, zero Claude quota cost). Use this subagent_type when the user asks to delegate a task, search, code change, or analysis to DeepSeek. IMPORTANT: Omit the 'model' parameter when calling Agent so this definition's model is preserved; you may set 'effort' (low, medium, high) if requested. ([nullptrowo/claude-router](https://github.com/nullptrowo/claude-router/blob/HEAD/files/agents/deepseek.md) · ★3)
- **deepseek**: DeepSeek V4 Flash via OpenCode Go. Subscription-cheap fallback worker. Use when the user says deepseek, opencode-go, or flash-deepseek. ([Zireael26/trellis](https://github.com/Zireael26/trellis/blob/HEAD/core-rules/omp/global/agents/deepseek.md) · ★1)
- **deepseek**: Transport Deepseek des avocats du diable — assemble mission + inputs étiquetés, appelle claude CLI sur ollama cloud (deepseek-v4.1-flash:cloud[1m]), retourne l'enveloppe JSON. L'exercice est porté par la mission fournie. ([eRom/erom-devil](https://github.com/eRom/erom-devil/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/deepseek.md))
- **deepseek**: Hands an implementation, debugging or research task to DeepSeek specifically (DeepSeek Harness / dsh, with the model configured for it) and reviews what it did. Use ONLY when the user names DeepSeek or dsh for the task; for a hand-off without a named engine use claude-workers:worker (Cursor while its quota lasts). Brief it like a contractor who has not seen the conversation - the goal, the absolute project directory, which files it may and may not touch, and how to check the result. ([lushuyu/claude-workers](https://github.com/lushuyu/claude-workers/blob/HEAD/plugins/claude-workers/agents/deepseek.md))
- **deepseek**: DeepSeek V4.1 Flash 1017 (`commandcode/deepseek/deepseek-v4.1-flash`, 320K ctx, vision). NEVER use in gpai-monorepo or with credentials/customer data. Outside gpai-monorepo: active worker alongside `sol` and `swe` for bug fixes, fill-in/normal implementation (DeepSWE 77.0%), scans, vision, and independent 4th-family reviews/verification. Effort: `lo` for fill-in/scans (-60% output tokens), omit (`high`) for normal coding/reviews, `hi` (`max`) for hard bug-hunting. Role via packet `Role:` line (skill://agent-orchestration). ([sharosoo/dotfile](https://github.com/sharosoo/dotfile/blob/HEAD/omp/agent/agents/deepseek.md))
- **deepseek**: MUST BE USED when the user names DeepSeek Pro — "ask deepseek", "ask agent deepseek", "use deepseek", "what would deepseek say", "deepseek's take". Not for multi-file implementation — use coder instead. Not for a multi-model panel — use advisors instead. ([whichguy/c-thru](https://github.com/whichguy/c-thru/blob/HEAD/agents/deepseek.md))
- **deepseek-agent**: Use this agent to delegate tasks to DeepSeek — code review, test generation, rescue tasks (implementing features, refactors), quick scripts, or freeform questions. Prefer deepseek-reasoner for hard reasoning tasks (complex refactors, architectural decisions, tricky edge case generation). Use deepseek-chat for everything else. ([aivsomkar/deepseek-plugin-cc](https://github.com/aivsomkar/deepseek-plugin-cc/blob/HEAD/agents/deepseek-agent.md) · ★1)

- **deepseek-flash**: Cheap DeepSeek route (third family; needs the utraque proxy). Pass effort on every call (low, medium, or high only): low for mechanical work or summaries; medium for bounded work with objective checks; high for leaf work spanning several files. At high, it takes security or sysadmin work that classifiers often block (vulnerability research, exploit testing, scanning Craig's own network, CA certificates) directly, and is the last step after other families refuse a task; never send it private keys or credentials. Verify consequential output with a stronger route. ([hughescr/claude-code-config](https://github.com/hughescr/claude-code-config/blob/HEAD/agents/deepseek-flash.md) · ★1)

- **Deepwork**: Runs a goal as kanban tickets: plans them as subtasks of one run ticket, gives each ticket a lead that runs it through its phases with its own sub-agents, fans the leads out in bounded waves and in through one state barrier per wave, and loops in bounded rounds until done, dry, stalled, out of rounds or over budget. A code goal gets the dev flow: autonomous reversible decisions with optional design/plan approval, parallel implementation tickets in isolated worktrees, followed by tests, verification and review once per integrated wave, a branch review, and a kept branch unless you explicitly c… ([iii-hq/workers](https://github.com/iii-hq/workers/blob/HEAD/kits/deepwork/agents/deepwork.md) · ★114)
- **Deepwork Adversary**: Hidden check tier: verifies plain work and verifies or reviews aggregate code batches. Same contract as deepwork-verifier, with less reasoning. Don't pin a model here: review runs on this tier too. To run only the verify phase on a lighter model, set the run's verify_model. ([iii-hq/workers](https://github.com/iii-hq/workers/blob/HEAD/kits/deepwork/agents/deepwork-adversary.md) · ★114)
- **Deepwork Implementer**: Hidden work tier for code cards and aggregate batches: implements each card without per-card checks, then adds aggregate coverage and repairs for the batch. Uses guarded file writes and explicit-path commits. Same base contract as deepwork-worker, with more reasoning. Pin a strong model here. ([iii-hq/workers](https://github.com/iii-hq/workers/blob/HEAD/kits/deepwork/agents/deepwork-impl.md) · ★114)
- **Deepwork Lead**: Hidden lead: runs one code card without per-card checks, one plain work card, or one aggregate batch that adds coverage and checks every integrated code card together. Retries rejected aggregate work and writes one result for the deepwork orchestrator. Never does ticket work itself. Pin a fast, cheap model here. ([iii-hq/workers](https://github.com/iii-hq/workers/blob/HEAD/kits/deepwork/agents/deepwork-lead.md) · ★114)
- **Deepwork Verifier**: Hidden adversarial leaf: verifies a plain work card, verifies or reviews an aggregate code batch, and performs the final completeness critic. Returns one verdict without ticket comments. Base of every check tier; must resolve no model so judges can pick one per spawn. ([iii-hq/workers](https://github.com/iii-hq/workers/blob/HEAD/kits/deepwork/agents/deepwork-verifier.md) · ★114)
- **Deepwork Worker**: Hidden leaf: does one card phase or aggregate batch work phase, returns one result document without ticket comments, then stops. Never edits ticket fields. Base of every work tier (`deepwork-worker*`, `deepwork-impl*`). ([iii-hq/workers](https://github.com/iii-hq/workers/blob/HEAD/kits/deepwork/agents/deepwork-worker.md) · ★114)

- **definition-of-done**: An agent's work is done when the code is proven, not when the agent says so. Every check below reads a proof that the tool which ran the check wrote itself. `scripts/devenv/dod.py` runs the check, and a Claude Code Stop hook runs it at the end of every turn, so an agent cannot finish a task that fails it. ([Nirmaan-app/nirmaan_stack_frappe15_postgres_14](https://github.com/Nirmaan-app/nirmaan_stack_frappe15_postgres_14/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/definition-of-done.md) · ★5)
- **definition-of-done**: Runs steps 1 and 2 of the Definition of Done — `./gradlew build`, then `./gradlew detekt` — and returns the verdict: two lines on success, the failing tests and their assertion output verbatim on failure, the failure class when the failure is infrastructural. Runs commands and reads files; changes nothing. Invoke it at the end of an issue, never for the test runs of a TDD cycle. ([AGRFesta/smart-home-api](https://github.com/AGRFesta/smart-home-api/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/definition-of-done.md))
- **definition-of-done**: A standing, project-wide bar that every change must clear before it counts as done. Unlike acceptance criteria, which vary per task and answer "did we build the right thing?", the Definition of Done is the same every time and answers "is this finished to our standard?". Apply it during planning, implementation and delivery, together with the configured validation gate. ([design4pro/planned.travel](https://github.com/design4pro/planned.travel/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/definition-of-done.md))
- **definition-of-done**: Một story chỉ được tính là **Done** (`status:done`) khi đạt mọi điều kiện dưới đây. DoD là cam kết chung của team; cùng với [Definition of Ready](process.md) (R1–R11) nó tạo thành hai đầu của mỗi story. ([phoenixit99/wata-ai-demo](https://github.com/phoenixit99/wata-ai-demo/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/definition-of-done.md) · 2 copies)
- **definition-of-done**: A change counts as done when all of this is true and you can say so from output you saw, not from memory. ([siroroman/BreathApp](https://github.com/siroroman/BreathApp/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/definition-of-done.md))
- **definition-of-done**: Every ticket in this repo inherits this. It is stated **once, here**, and no ticket body repeats it. ([tamasmakos/processrecall](https://github.com/tamasmakos/processrecall/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/definition-of-done.md))

Use for any delegate-agents task so run output stays out of the main conversation. ([ArunPrakashG/delegate-agents](https://github.com/ArunPrakashG/delegate-agents/blob/HEAD/agents/delegate.md))
- **delegate**: Hand a self-contained coding, review or investigation task to Codex, Antigravity, OpenRouter or local Ollama, picked by live usage, to save Claude quota. Use when told to delegate, or when Claude's usage is high and a task can run without this conversation. Workers are offline: a task that needs the network, an MCP server, a connector or the browser stays with the caller. The prompt must be a complete brief and say write or read. ([CornelB/SkyNET-agents](https://github.com/CornelB/SkyNET-agents/blob/HEAD/agents/delegate.md))
- **delegate**: The agent an owner's one-off delegated work runs as — research, an investigation, a design or plan, a bounded change. Reach for it when the work is multi-step and needs to WRITE something (a document, a change, a commit); reach for Explore instead when the answer is a read-only search, and for a posture agent (architect, developer, cpo …) when the task wants that role's loader stack and its Role on the commit. Carries no posture, no loader and no inbox — the task prompt is the whole assignment. Pins NO model on purpose, because the tier is a per-run choice the spawner resolves through model_a… ([eyalgo/kensaiapp](https://github.com/eyalgo/kensaiapp/blob/HEAD/skills/interactive/.claude/agents/delegate.md))
- **delegate**: Despachador de la flota externa (Sonnet) que ejecuta encargos en opencode, antigravity o devin y absorbe sus logs en su propio contexto. Úsalo cuando vayas a delegar trabajo a una CLI externa y no quieras que miles de líneas de output entren en el contexto principal. ([Gdearmascasta/Dotfiles](https://github.com/Gdearmascasta/Dotfiles/blob/HEAD/agentes/claude/agents/delegate.md))
- **delegate**: Sonnet worker for ONE well-specified task defined in a brief file — builds, tests, renders, scripts, searches, code changes. Use for any execution work the supervising session must not run itself; the spawn prompt names the brief path. ([jgengineering2000/aur-ai-ux-refinements](https://github.com/jgengineering2000/aur-ai-ux-refinements/blob/HEAD/agents/delegate.md))
- **delegate**: General-purpose agent for handling commits and PRs. Always fetches latest conventions before starting. ([kolchurinvv/kolchurin.dev](https://github.com/kolchurinvv/kolchurin.dev/blob/HEAD/agents/delegate.md))
- **delegate**: Hands one task to another CLI harness (opencode, omp, pi, zcode, claude and any other in the huddle table) in the current folder (or the one `--dir` names) and returns the harness's report unchanged. Use when the user or the main thread wants a task done by a named harness, or a second implementation from another agent harness. Give it the harness id and the task. ([MuhmdRaouf/huddle](https://github.com/MuhmdRaouf/huddle/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/delegate.md))
- **delegate**: Delegate subagent for code-writing and helper tasks spawned by the lead session. Use for delegates, not as the runner of a playbook itself. Spawn a fresh `delegate` for each new task, and resume one only in the strict cases that the workflow skill's Subagents section names. Reads the `workflow` skill's `SKILL.md` in full before any work, including its inline Principles index. Substituting a general-purpose subagent skips that read and drifts. ([shreshthkapai/agent-workflows](https://github.com/shreshthkapai/agent-workflows/blob/HEAD/agents/delegate.md))
- **delegate**: Break a bounded task into independent pieces and synthesize delegated results. ([sonwjnn/wincode](https://github.com/sonwjnn/wincode/blob/HEAD/packages/subagents/agents/delegate.md))
- **delegate**: Does one bounded job in a tblock-mode run and ends with a fixed report. Spawned by tblock-mode for code changes, fixes, and helper tasks; never runs tblock-mode itself. ([tommyc10/tblock](https://github.com/tommyc10/tblock/blob/HEAD/agents/delegate.md) · 2 copies)
- **delegate**: Delegate subagent for code and helper tasks during a `/astack:mode` run. Use for delegates, not as the runner for `/astack:mode` itself. - **delegate-coordinator**: Coordinator role for the orchestrated-delegation workflow. The lead consults this as a session-level steer that keeps the run aimed at the goal. - **delegate-executor**: Executor role for the orchestrated-delegation workflow, and the Dark Factory's `build` stage. - **delegate-facts**: Read-only fetching for the lead -- locate a file or line, extract a quoted value, or run named read-only commands and report their output, every fact with its source. - **delivery-coordinator**: Emre — the Guild's delivery coordinator.
